  function generateImagingRequestPrintHtml(order) {
    const demo = resolvePatientDemographics(order?.request?.patientSnapshot || patient);
    const now = new Date(order?.createdAt || Date.now());
    const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString('en-GB');
    const doctorName = order.requesterName || user?.displayName || user?.name || user?.username || 'គ្រូពេទ្យពិនិត្យ';
    return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="km">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Medical Imaging Request — មន្ទីរពេទ្យបង្អែក បរកែវ</title>
  <style>
    @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 12mm 15mm; }
    @font-face { font-family: 'Moul'; src: url('/public/fonts/Moul-Khmer.woff2') format('woff2'); }
    body { font-family: "Khmer OS Battambang", Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.45; color: #000; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
    .header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; border-bottom: 2px solid #000; padding-bottom: 10px; margin-bottom: 14px; }
    .moul-title { font-family: 'Moul', serif; font-size: 16px; margin-top: 4px; }
    .box { border: 1.5px solid #000; padding: 10px 14px; margin-bottom: 14px; }
    .row { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px 16px; margin-bottom: 6px; }
    .val { font-weight: 700; border-bottom: 1px dotted #333; padding: 0 4px; }
    .details { border: 1.5px solid #000; padding: 12px 14px; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="header">
    <div>
      <img src="/public/logo-hospital.jpg" style="height:55px;width:55px;border-radius:50%;object-fit:contain;">
      <div class="moul-title">មន្ទីរពេទ្យបង្អែក បរកែវ</div>
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:right;">
      <h2 style="margin:0;font-size:17px;">ប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំរូបភាពវេជ្ជសាស្ត្រ</h2>
      <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;">Medical Imaging Request Form</div>
      <div style="font-size:11px;margin-top:4px;">No: ${esc(order.accessionNumber || '')} · ${esc(order.priority || 'ROUTINE')}</div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="box">
    <div class="row">
      <span>ឈ្មោះអ្នកជំងឺ: <strong class="val">${esc(demo.name)}</strong></span>
      <span>អាយុ: <span class="val">${esc(demo.age)}</span></span>
      <span>ភេទ: <span class="val">${esc(demo.isFemale ? 'ស្រី' : demo.isMale ? 'ប្រុស' : '—')}</span></span>
      <span>ផ្នែក: <span class="val">${esc(demo.ward || '—')}</span></span>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span>អាសយដ្ឋាន: <span class="val">${esc(`${demo.village}, ${demo.commune}, ${demo.district}, ${demo.province}`)}</span></span>
      <span>ទូរស័ព្ទ: <span class="val">${esc(demo.phone)}</span></span>
    </div>
    <div class="row">
      <span>រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ: <span class="val" style="flex:1;">${esc(order.request?.clinicalIndication || demo.diag || '—')}</span></span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="details">
    <h3 style="margin:0 0 10px;border-bottom:1px solid #ccc;padding-bottom:4px;">ព័ត៌មានសំណើ</h3>
    <div style="font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>ផ្នែក:</strong> ${esc(modalityLabel(order.modality))} [${esc(order.modality)}]</div>
    <div style="font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px;"><strong>ការពិនិត្យ:</strong> ${esc(order.request?.examination || order.request?.bodyArea || '—')}</div>
    ${order.request?.projection ? `<div><strong>Projection:</strong> ${esc(order.request.projection)}</div>` : ''}
    ${order.request?.contrast ? `<div><strong>Contrast:</strong> ${esc(order.request.contrast)}</div>` : ''}
    ${order.request?.clinicalIndication ? `<div style="margin-top:8px;"><strong>Clinical Indication:</strong> ${esc(order.request.clinicalIndication)}</div>` : ''}
    ${order.request?.history ? `<div><strong>Relevant History:</strong> ${esc(order.request.history)}</div>` : ''}
    ${order.request?.question ? `<div><strong>Question for Radiology:</strong> ${esc(order.request.question)}</div>` : ''}
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:30px;padding:0 20px;">
    <div>
      <div>កាលបរិច្ឆេទស្នើសុំ: ${esc(dateStr)}</div>
    </div>
    <div style="text-align:right;">
      <div>ហត្ថលេខា និង ឈ្មោះគ្រូពេទ្យ:</div>
      ${requisitionSignatureHtml(order.request?.requesterSignature, doctorName)}
    </div>
  </div>
</body>
</html>`;
  }

  function printOrderRequisition(order) {
    if (!order) return;
    if (order.modality === 'LAB') {
      const html = generateLabRequestPrintHtml({ order });
      printHtmlContent(html);
    } else {
      const html = generateImagingRequestPrintHtml(order);
      printHtmlContent(html);
    }
  }

  async function openLabOrder(context, orderPatientKey) {
    const rawTests = await getCatalog();
    const tests = rawTests.filter(test => test.active !== false);
    const demo = resolvePatientDemographics();
    const now = new Date();
    const dateStr = now.toLocaleDateString('en-GB');
    const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const doctorName = user?.displayName || user?.name || user?.username || 'គ្រូពេទ្យពិនិត្យ';
    const wardName = context === 'opd' ? 'ផ្នែកពិគ្រោះជំងឺក្រៅ (OPD)' : (demo.ward || '—');

    const rawPmrs = String(demo.pmrs || '').replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z]/g, '');
    const digits = rawPmrs.padEnd(10, ' ').slice(0, 10).split('');
    const pmrsBoxesHtml = digits.map(d => `<span class="pmrs-box">${esc(d.trim())}</span>`).join('');

    const activeCodes = new Set(tests.map(test => test.code));
    function testRow(code, name, extraAttrs = '') {
      if (!activeCodes.has(code)) return '';
      const duplicate = hasExistingLabTest(code);
      const extra = /data-extra-test="([^"]+)"/.exec(extraAttrs)?.[1];
      if (extra && !activeCodes.has(extra)) return '';
      const disabled = duplicate ? 'disabled' : '';
      return `<div class="test-check-row">
        <label class="test-label">
          <input type="checkbox" value="${esc(code)}" data-paper-test ${extraAttrs} ${disabled}>
          <span>${esc(name)}</span>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="test-star-urgent" data-star-code="${esc(code)}" ${disabled} title="បន្ទាន់ (Urgent)">☆</button>
      </div>`;
    }

    const formHtml = `<form class="paraclinic-form borkeo-lab-paper-form">
      <!-- Hidden compatibility anchors for existing tests -->
      <div style="display:none;" data-cat-select="__all__" data-active-category-title></div>

      <!-- Header of Paper Requisition Sheet -->
      <div class="lab-paper-header">
        <div class="lab-paper-logo-block">
          <img src="/public/logo-hospital.jpg" alt="Logo" class="lab-paper-logo">
          <div class="hospital-name-moul">មន្ទីរពេទ្យបង្អែក បរកែវ</div>
        </div>
        <div class="lab-paper-title-block">
          <div class="lab-paper-title-kh">ប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំវិភាគតេស្តមន្ទីរពិសោធន៍</div>
          <div class="lab-paper-title-en">Laboratory Request Form</div>
        </div>
        <div class="lab-paper-codes-block">
          <div class="lab-code-row">
            <span>លេខកូដមន្ទីរពិសោធន៍:</span>
            <input type="text" name="labCode" class="paper-input-box" placeholder="Lab Code">
          </div>
          <div class="pmrs-code-row">
            <span>PMRS CODE:</span>
            <div class="pmrs-boxes">${pmrsBoxesHtml}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Patient Information Bar -->
      <div class="lab-paper-patient-card">
        <div class="patient-line-1">
          <span class="lbl">ឈ្មោះអ្នកជំងឺ:</span> <strong class="val name-val">${esc(demo.name)}</strong>
          <span class="lbl">អាយុ:</span> <span class="val age-val">${esc(demo.age)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">ថ្ងៃខែឆ្នាំកំណើត:</span> <span class="val dob-val">${esc(demo.dob)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">ភេទ:</span>
          <label class="check-inline"><input type="checkbox" disabled ${demo.isFemale ? 'checked' : ''}> ស្រី</label>
          <label class="check-inline"><input type="checkbox" disabled ${demo.isMale ? 'checked' : ''}> ប្រុស</label>
        </div>
        <div class="patient-line-2">
          <span class="lbl">អាសយដ្ឋានអ្នកជំងឺ:</span>
          <span>ភូមិ</span> <span class="val">${esc(demo.village)}</span>
          <span>ឃុំ</span> <span class="val">${esc(demo.commune)}</span>
          <span>ស្រុក</span> <span class="val">${esc(demo.district)}</span>
          <span>ខេត្ត</span> <span class="val">${esc(demo.province)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="patient-line-3">
          <span class="lbl">ផ្នែក:</span> <span class="val dept-val">${esc(wardName)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">រោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ:</span> <input type="text" name="clinicalDiagnosis" class="paper-line-input diag-val" value="${esc(demo.diag)}" placeholder="បញ្ចូលរោគវិនិច្ឆ័យ">
        </div>
        <div class="patient-line-4">
          <span class="lbl">កាលបរិច្ឆេទស្រង់វត្ថុវិភាគ:</span> <span class="val">${esc(dateStr)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">ម៉ោង:</span> <span class="val">${esc(timeStr)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">ឈ្មោះអ្នកស្រង់:</span> <span class="val">${esc(doctorName)}</span>
          <span class="lbl">ទូរស័ព្ទ:</span> <span class="val">${esc(demo.phone)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Authentic 3-Column Checklist Grid Matching Hospital Paper Form -->
      <div class="lab-paper-grid">
        <!-- Col 1: HEMATOLOGY -->
        <div class="lab-col col-1">
          <div class="col-head">HEMATOLOGY</div>
          <div class="specimen-tag purple">SPECIMEN: Purple/blue top tube</div>
          ${testRow('CBC', 'Complete Blood Count (CBC)')}

          <div class="col-subhead">OTHER HEMATOLOGY TESTS</div>
          ${testRow('RETIC', 'Reticulocyte')}
          ${testRow('MALARIA_SMEAR', 'Malaria ( D1, D2, C1, C1 )')}
          ${testRow('ESR', 'ESR/VS')}
          ${testRow('BLOOD_FILM', 'Blood cell morphology')}
        </div>

        <!-- Col 2: HEMATO- IMMUNOLOGY / HEMOSTASIS / SERO- IMMUNOLOGY / URINE & STOOL -->
        <div class="lab-col col-2">
          <div class="col-head">HEMATO- IMMUNOLOGY</div>
          <div class="specimen-tag purple">SPECIMEN: Purple/blue top tube</div>
          ${testRow('BLOOD_GROUP', 'ABO and Rh blood group')}

          <div class="col-subhead">HEMOSTASIS</div>
          <div class="specimen-tag text-dark">SPECIMEN: Direct bleed lab staff</div>
          ${testRow('COAG_PROFILE', 'Bleeding Time (Ts)')}
          ${testRow('COAG_PROFILE', 'Clotting Time (Tc)')}
          <div class="specimen-tag green">SPECIMEN: Green top tube</div>
          ${testRow('PT', 'PT (Prothrombin Time)')}
          ${testRow('INR', 'INR (International Normalized Ratio)')}
          ${testRow('APTT', 'aPTT (Activated partial thromboplastin time)')}

          <div class="col-subhead">SERO- IMMUNOLOGY</div>
          <div class="specimen-tag red">SPECIMEN: Red top tube</div>
          ${testRow('HBSAG', 'HBs antigen')}
          ${testRow('HBV_RDT', 'HBs antibody')}
          ${testRow('HCV', 'HCV antibody')}
          ${testRow('HIV', 'HIV antibody (screening)')}
          ${testRow('RPR', 'Syphilis RPR antibody')}
          ${testRow('TPHA', 'Syphilis TPHA antibody')}
          ${testRow('ASO', 'ASO (antistreptolysin-o)')}
          ${testRow('CRP', 'CRP (C-Reactive Protein) semi qt')}
          ${testRow('CRP', 'CRP test quantitative')}
          ${testRow('H_PYLORI_RDT', 'H.Pylori antibody')}
          ${testRow('DENGUE_NS1', 'Dengue NS1Ag/IgM/IgG')}

          <div class="col-subhead">URINE & STOOL ANALYSIS</div>
          ${testRow('UA', 'Chemistry (pH, Protein, Glucose)')}
          ${testRow('UA', 'Sediment')}
          ${testRow('PREG_RDT', 'Pregnancy test')}
          ${testRow('STOOL_EXAM', 'Stool Microscopy')}
        </div>

        <!-- Col 3: BIOCHEMISTRY -->
        <div class="lab-col col-3">
          <div class="col-head">BIOCHEMISTRY</div>
          <div class="specimen-tag red">SPECIMEN: Red top tube</div>
          ${testRow('GLU', 'Glucose (fasting/Random)')}
          ${testRow('CA', 'Calcium')}
          ${testRow('URIC', 'Uric acid')}

          <div class="col-subhead">Renal Function</div>
          ${testRow('UREA', 'Urea')}
          ${testRow('CREAT', 'Creatinine')}
          ${testRow('ELECTROLYTE_PROFILE', 'Electrolytes (Na+, K+, Cl-)')}

          <div class="col-subhead">Liver Function</div>
          ${testRow('TBIL', 'Bilirubin Direct & Total')}
          ${testRow('AST', 'Transaminase (AST/GOT, ALT/GPT)', 'data-extra-test="ALT"')}
          ${testRow('GGT', 'Gamma GT')}
          ${testRow('ALP', 'PAL (Phosphatase alkaline)')}
          ${testRow('TP', 'Total protein')}
          ${testRow('ALB', 'Albumin')}

          <div class="col-subhead">Lipid Profile</div>
          ${testRow('CHOL', 'Cholesterol')}
          ${testRow('HDL', 'HDL Cholesterol')}
          ${testRow('LDL', 'LDL Cholesterol')}
          ${testRow('TG', 'Triglycerides')}

          <div class="col-subhead">Pancreatic enzyme</div>
          ${testRow('AMYLASE', 'Amylase')}
          ${testRow('LIPASE', 'Lipase')}

          <div class="col-subhead">Other test</div>
          <div class="other-test-picker" style="margin-top:2px;">
            <select class="paper-select" data-other-test-select style="width:100%;padding:4px;font-size:11px;border:1px solid #94a3b8;border-radius:4px;">
              <option value="">+ ជ្រើសរើសតេស្តបន្ថែម...</option>
              ${tests.map(t => `<option value="${esc(t.code)}">${esc(t.name)} (${esc(t.code)})</option>`).join('')}
            </select>
            <div data-other-tests-container style="margin-top:4px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Footer matching paper sheet -->
      <div class="lab-paper-footer">
        <div class="footer-box">
          <div>លក្ខណៈវត្ថុវិភាគ ត្រឹមត្រូវ [ ] អាចទទួលយកបាន [ ] បដិសេធ [ ]</div>
          <div>កាលបរិច្ឆេទទទួលវត្ថុវិភាគ ............ ម៉ោង ............</div>
          <div>ហត្ថលេខា និង ឈ្មោះអ្នកទទួល .................................................</div>
        </div>
        <div class="footer-box" style="text-align:right;">
          <div>កាលបរិច្ឆេទស្នើសុំ ថ្ងៃទី ${now.getDate()} ខែ ${now.getMonth() + 1} ឆ្នាំ ${now.getFullYear()}</div>
          <div style="margin-top:6px;">ហត្ថលេខា និង ឈ្មោះគ្រូពេទ្យ:</div>
          ${requisitionSignatureHtml(null, doctorName)}
        </div>
      </div>

      ${opdFeeChoice(context)}

      <div class="lab-paper-actions-bar">
        <div class="actions-left">
          <button type="button" class="btn-paper-print" data-preview-print>🖨️ បោះពុម្ពប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំ</button>
        </div>
        <div class="actions-right">
          <button type="button" data-cancel>បោះបង់</button>
          ${opdSubmitActions(context)}
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>`;

    const view = modal('ប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំវិភាគតេស្តមន្ទីរពិសោធន៍ (Laboratory Request Form)', formHtml, 'borkeo-lab-paper-modal');
    const form = view.body.querySelector('form');
    view.body.querySelector('[data-cancel]').addEventListener('click', view.close);

    view.body.querySelectorAll('.test-star-urgent').forEach(starBtn => {
      starBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        const rowEl = starBtn.closest('.test-check-row');
        const chkBox = rowEl?.querySelector('[data-paper-test]');
        const isActive = starBtn.classList.toggle('active');
        starBtn.textContent = isActive ? '★' : '☆';
        if (isActive && chkBox && !chkBox.checked) {
          chkBox.checked = true;
          chkBox.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        }
      });
    });

    const otherContainer = view.body.querySelector('[data-other-tests-container]');
    const otherSelect = view.body.querySelector('[data-other-test-select]');
    otherSelect?.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const code = otherSelect.value;
      if (!code) return;
      if (view.body.querySelector(`[data-paper-test][value="${CSS.escape(code)}"]`)) {
        alert('Test already in requisition form.');
        otherSelect.value = '';
        return;
      }
      const testObj = tests.find(t => t.code === code);
      const label = testObj ? `${testObj.name} (${testObj.code})` : code;
      const rowEl = document.createElement('div');
      rowEl.className = 'test-check-row';
      rowEl.innerHTML = `<label class="test-label"><input type="checkbox" value="${esc(code)}" data-paper-test checked><span>${esc(label)}</span></label><button type="button" class="test-star-urgent" data-star-code="${esc(code)}" title="បន្ទាន់ (Urgent)">☆</button><button type="button" data-remove-other style="border:0;background:transparent;color:#dc2626;cursor:pointer;padding:0 4px;font-size:14px;">×</button>`;
      rowEl.querySelector('[data-remove-other]').addEventListener('click', () => rowEl.remove());
      rowEl.querySelector('.test-star-urgent').addEventListener('click', e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        const btn = rowEl.querySelector('.test-star-urgent');
        const active = btn.classList.toggle('active');
        btn.textContent = active ? '★' : '☆';
      });
      otherContainer.appendChild(rowEl);
      otherSelect.value = '';
    });

    const getSelectedItems = () => {
      const checkedBoxes = [...view.body.querySelectorAll('[data-paper-test]:checked')];
      const urgentCodes = new Set(
        [...view.body.querySelectorAll('.test-star-urgent.active')].map(b => b.dataset.starCode)
      );
      const set = new Set();
      checkedBoxes.forEach(cb => {
        set.add(cb.value);
        if (cb.dataset.extraTest) set.add(cb.dataset.extraTest);
      });
      const items = [...set].map(code => ({
        code,
        urgent: urgentCodes.has(code)
      }));
      const isUrgent = items.some(i => i.urgent);
      return {
        codes: set,
        urgentCodes,
        items,
        isUrgent
      };
    };

    // Immediate Print Requisition Form Preview button
    view.body.querySelector('[data-preview-print]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const { codes, urgentCodes, isUrgent } = getSelectedItems();
      const labCodeVal = form.elements.labCode?.value || '';
      const diagVal = form.elements.clinicalDiagnosis?.value || '';
      const printHtml = generateLabRequestPrintHtml({
        checkedCodes: codes,
        urgentCodes,
        clinicalDiagnosis: diagVal,
        priority: isUrgent ? 'URGENT' : 'ROUTINE',
        labCode: labCodeVal,
        customDoctor: doctorName
      });
      printHtmlContent(printHtml);
    });

    form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const { items, isUrgent } = getSelectedItems();
      if (!items.length) {
        alert('សូមជ្រើសរើសតេស្តមន្ទីរពិសោធន៍យ៉ាងហោចណាស់មួយ');
        return;
      }
      const formData = new FormData(form);
      const priority = isUrgent ? 'URGENT' : (formData.get('priority') || 'ROUTINE');
      const clinicalDiagnosis = form.elements.clinicalDiagnosis?.value || '';
      const labCodeVal = form.elements.labCode?.value || '';

      try {
        const result = await json('/api/paraclinic/orders', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            patientKey: orderPatientKey,
            modality: 'LAB',
            priority,
            chargeMode: formData.get('chargeMode') || '',
            handoffMode: event.submitter?.dataset.route || 'DIRECT',
            opdInvestigationText: document.getElementById('inv_list')?.value || '',
            request: {
              clinicalInfo: clinicalDiagnosis,
              diagnosis: clinicalDiagnosis,
              labCode: labCodeVal,
              items
            }
          }),
        });

        if (!applyInvestigationText(result.investigationText, context)) {
          appendSelection('LAB', { items }, context, orderPatientKey);
        }

        view.close();
        refresh();

        if (confirm('សំណើបានបញ្ជូនជោគជ័យ! តើលោកអ្នកចង់បោះពុម្ពប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំផ្លូវការឥឡូវនេះដែរឬទេ?')) {
          printOrderRequisition(result.order || {
            modality: 'LAB',
            priority,
            accessionNumber: result.accessionNumber,
            requesterName: doctorName,
            request: { clinicalInfo: clinicalDiagnosis, items }
          });
        }

        if (result.cashierPaymentRequired) alert('បានបញ្ជូនទៅបញ្ជរបង់ប្រាក់');
        else if (result.nurseHandoffRequired) alert('Order sent to the OPD nurse. The nurse will collect the sample and send it to Laboratory.');
        else if (result.chargeMode === 'FREE') alert('Paraclinic order marked free of charge and sent directly to Laboratory.');
      } catch (error) {
        alert(error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  function labWorkflowHtml(order) {
    const steps = [
      ['Doctor Ordered', order.createdAt, order.requesterName],
      ['Ward Received', order.wardReceivedAt, order.wardReceiverName],
      ['Specimen Collected', order.collectedAt, order.collectorName],
      ['Sent to Laboratory', order.sentToLabAt, order.senderName],
      ['Laboratory Received', order.receivedAt, order.receiverName],
      ['Result Released to Ward', order.releasedAt, order.validatorName],
      ['Ward Seen', order.wardSeenAt, order.wardSeenName],
    ];
    return `<div class="ward-lab-timeline">${steps.map(([name,time,by]) => `<div class="ward-lab-step ${time ? 'done' : ''}"><strong>${esc(name)}</strong><span>${time ? new Date(time).toLocaleString('en-GB') : 'Pending'}</span>${time && by ? `<small>${esc(by)}</small>` : ''}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }

  function resultRows(order) {
    const groups = groupLabItems(order.result?.items || []);
    return [...groups].map(([category, rows]) => `<section class="ward-result-group"><h3>${esc(categoryLabel(category))}</h3>${rows.map(({labTest,...result}) => {
      const short = shortTestName(labTest) || result.name || result.code;
      const full = labTest?.name && short !== labTest.name ? `<small>${esc(labTest.name)}</small>` : '';
      const flag = result.flag;
      const flagText = flag === 'LOW' ? '▼ Low' : flag === 'HIGH' ? '▲ High' : flag === 'CRITICAL_LOW' ? '⚠️▼ Crit Low' : flag === 'CRITICAL_HIGH' ? '⚠️▲ Crit High' : '';
      const flagBadge = flagText ? `<span class="lab-flag-badge flag-${esc(flag.toLowerCase())}">${esc(flagText)}</span>` : '';
      return `<div class="result-item">
        <strong>${esc(short)}${full}</strong><span class="result-value ${result.flag ? 'abnormal-result' : ''}">${esc(result.value ?? '')} ${esc(result.unit || '')}</span>
        ${flagBadge}
        <span>${result.referenceLow !== null && result.referenceLow !== undefined ? `${esc(result.referenceLow)}–${esc(result.referenceHigh ?? '')}` : ''}</span>
        </div>`;
    }).join('')}</section>`).join('');
  }

  async function updateLabWardStage(order, status, extra = {}) {
    await json(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${order.id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ status, ...extra }),
    });
    await refresh();
  }

  function openCollectionDialog(order, replacement = false) {
    const view = modal(replacement ? 'Recollect Specimen' : 'Collect Specimen', `<form class="paraclinic-form">
      <div class="collection-alert ${replacement ? 'danger' : ''}">${replacement ? `<strong>Previous specimen was rejected:</strong> ${esc(order.rejectionReason || '')}` : 'Select the specimen collection date and time. The current time is pre-filled.'}</div>
      <label>Specimen Collection Date and Time<input type="datetime-local" name="collectedLocal" value="${localDateTimeValue()}" required></label>
      <div class="paraclinic-submit"><button type="button" data-cancel>Cancel</button><button type="submit" class="primary">Confirm Specimen Collection</button></div>
    </form>`);
    const form = view.body.querySelector('form');
    view.body.querySelector('[data-cancel]').addEventListener('click', view.close);
    form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const value = form.elements.collectedLocal.value;
      const collectedAt = new Date(value).getTime();
      if (!Number.isFinite(collectedAt)) { alert('Please select a valid specimen collection date and time.'); return; }
      try {
        await updateLabWardStage(order, 'COLLECTED', { collectedAt });
        view.close();
      } catch (error) { alert(error.message); }
    });
  }

  async function openResult(id) {
    try {
      const order = (await json(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${id}`)).order;
      if (order.modality === 'LAB') await getCatalog();
      let content = `<div class="result-meta"><strong>${esc(order.accessionNumber || '')}</strong><span>${esc(priorityLabel(order.priority))} · ${esc(statusLabel(order.status))}</span></div>`;
      if (order.modality === 'LAB') content += labWorkflowHtml(order);
      if (order.status === 'REJECTED') content += `<div class="result-rejected"><strong>Specimen Rejected:</strong> ${esc(order.rejectionReason || 'No reason recorded.')}<br><small>${order.rejectedAt ? new Date(order.rejectedAt).toLocaleString('en-GB') : ''} ${order.rejectorName ? `· ${esc(order.rejectorName)}` : ''}</small></div>`;
      if (order.modality === 'LAB') {
        const orderedGroups = groupLabItems(order.request?.items || []);
        content += `<section class="ward-order-groups"><h3>Ordered Tests</h3>${[...orderedGroups].map(([category, rows]) => `<div class="ward-order-group"><strong>${esc(categoryLabel(category))}</strong><div>${rows.map(row => `<span class="test-chip ${row.urgent ? 'urgent' : ''}" title="${esc(row.labTest?.name || row.code)}">${row.urgent ? '★ ' : ''}${esc(shortTestName(row.labTest) || row.code)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')}</section>`;
        content += `<div class="ward-result-groups">${resultRows(order) || '<p>No released results yet.</p>'}</div>`;
        if (order.hasCritical) {
          content += `<div class="result-critical"><strong>Critical Laboratory Result</strong><br>${order.criticalAckAt ? `Acknowledged by ${esc(order.criticalAckName || '')} at ${new Date(order.criticalAckAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')}${order.criticalAckNote ? ` — ${esc(order.criticalAckNote)}` : ''}` : 'Doctor acknowledgment required.'}</div>`;
        }
      } else {
        content += `<h3>Findings</h3><p>${esc(order.result?.findings || '—')}</p><h3>Conclusion</h3><p>${esc(order.result?.conclusion || '—')}</p><h3>Recommendation</h3><p>${esc(order.result?.recommendation || '—')}</p>`;
      }
      content += `<div class="paraclinic-attachments"><h3>Attachments</h3>${(order.attachments || []).map(attachment => `<a href="/api/paraclinic/attachments/${attachment.id}" target="_blank">${esc(attachment.filename)}</a>`).join('') || '<p>None.</p>'}</div>`;

      if (order.modality === 'LAB' && isWardNursingRole()) {
        if (order.status === 'ORDERED') content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="primary" data-ward-receive>Receive Doctor Order</button></div>';
        if (order.status === 'WARD_RECEIVED') content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="primary" data-collect>Collect Specimen</button></div>';
        if (order.status === 'COLLECTED') content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="primary" data-send-lab>Send Specimen to Laboratory</button></div>';
        if (order.status === 'REJECTED') content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="critical-action" data-recollect>Recollect Specimen</button></div>';
        if (order.status === 'RESULT_AVAILABLE' && !order.wardSeenAt) content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="primary" data-ward-seen>Ward Mark as Seen</button></div>';
      }
      if (order.hasCritical && order.releasedAt && !order.criticalAckAt && (['DOCTOR','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(user?.activeRole))) content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="critical-action" data-critical-ack>Acknowledge Critical Result</button></div>';
      if (order.releasedAt && !order.reviewedAt && (['DOCTOR','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(user?.activeRole))) content += '<div class="paraclinic-submit"><button class="primary" data-review>Confirm Seen</button></div>';
      content += seenNotes(order);
      if (order.wardSeenAt) content += `<p class="ward-seen-note"><strong>Ward Seen:</strong> ${new Date(order.wardSeenAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')} by ${esc(order.wardSeenName || '')}</p>`;
      content += `<div class="paraclinic-print-actions" style="margin:14px 0;padding:10px 14px;background:#f0f9ff;border:1px solid #bae6fd;border-radius:8px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;">
        <div>
          <strong style="color:#0369a1;">ប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំផ្លូវការ</strong>
          <small style="display:block;color:#64748b;">អាចបោះពុម្ពប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំឡើងវិញគ្រប់ពេលវេលា ទោះបីជាសំណើត្រូវបានបិទ ឬចេញលទ្ធផលហើយក៏ដោយ</small>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="btn-blue" data-modal-print-requisition style="font-weight:700;padding:8px 16px;">🖨️ បោះពុម្ពប័ណ្ណស្នើសុំ</button>
      </div>`;

      const view = modal(`${order.releasedAt ? 'Result' : 'Request'} ${modalityLabel(order.modality)}`, content);
      view.body.querySelector('[data-modal-print-requisition]')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        printOrderRequisition(order);
      });
      // Opening a released report counts as seen only after the first explicit
      // acknowledgment. Worklists, background polling and printing never stamp it.
      if (order.releasedAt && order.reviewedAt && (['DOCTOR','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(user?.activeRole))) {
        json(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${order.id}/view`, {method:'POST'}).then(result => {
          view.body.querySelector('[data-result-seen]')?.replaceWith(seenNotesElement(result.order));
        }).catch(error => {
          const note=view.body.querySelector('[data-result-seen]');
          if (note) {const warning=document.createElement('p');warning.textContent=`Unable to record this result view: ${error.message}`;note.append(warning);}
        });
      }
      view.body.querySelector('[data-ward-receive]')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        try { await updateLabWardStage(order, 'WARD_RECEIVED'); view.close(); } catch (error) { alert(error.message); }
      });
      view.body.querySelector('[data-collect]')?.addEventListener('click', () => { view.close(); openCollectionDialog(order, false); });
      view.body.querySelector('[data-recollect]')?.addEventListener('click', () => { view.close(); openCollectionDialog(order, true); });
      view.body.querySelector('[data-send-lab]')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (!confirm('Send this collected specimen to the laboratory now?')) return;
        try { await updateLabWardStage(order, 'SENT_TO_LAB'); view.close(); } catch (error) { alert(error.message); }
      });
      view.body.querySelectorAll('[data-ward-seen]').forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        try { await updateLabWardStage(order, 'WARD_SEEN'); view.close(); } catch (error) { alert(error.message); }
      }));
      view.body.querySelector('[data-critical-ack]')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const note = prompt('Critical result acknowledgment note (optional):', '') ?? '';
        await json(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${order.id}/critical-ack`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ note }) });
        view.close(); await refresh();
      });
      view.body.querySelector('[data-review]')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const button=view.body.querySelector('[data-review]');button.disabled=true;
        try {
          const result=await json(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${order.id}/review`, { method: 'POST' });
          view.body.querySelector('[data-result-seen]')?.replaceWith(seenNotesElement(result.order));
          button.remove();await refresh();
        } catch(error) {button.disabled=false;alert(error.message);}
      });
    } catch (error) { alert(error.message); }
  }

  function seenNotes(order) {
    const views=order.seenBy?.length ? order.seenBy : order.reviewedAt
      ? [{doctorName:order.reviewerName,seenAt:order.reviewedAt}] : [];
    return `<section data-result-seen class="result-seen-history">${views.map(item =>
      `<p><strong>Seen by</strong> <span data-language-exclude>${esc(item.doctorName || '')}</span> <strong>at</strong> <time>${new Date(item.seenAt).toLocaleString('en-GB')}</time></p>`).join('')}</section>`;
  }

  function seenNotesElement(order) {
    const host=document.createElement('div');host.innerHTML=seenNotes(order);return host.firstElementChild;
  }

  function bootstrap(session) {
    user = session?.user;
    csrf = session?.csrfToken || csrf;
    if (canUse()) setup();
  }

  if (window.emrSession) bootstrap(window.emrSession);
  window.addEventListener('emr-session-ready', event => bootstrap(event.detail));
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-loaded', event => setPatient(event.detail?.patient));
  document.addEventListener('emr:patient-saved', event => setPatient(event.detail?.patient));
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => { const loaded = window.getLoadedEmrPatient?.(); if (loaded) setPatient(loaded); });
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => { if (timer) clearInterval(timer); });
})();

===== END FILE: public/paraclinic.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: public/report-print.js =====
'use strict';
// Print only the linked report in the browser's native preview. Never save or sign.
(() => {
  let activePrint = null;
  const bounded = (promise, milliseconds = 4000) => Promise.race([
    Promise.resolve(promise).catch(() => {}),
    new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds)),
  ]);
  const nextFrame = (win, fallbackMs = 50) => new Promise(resolve => {
    let done = false;
    const finish = () => { if (!done) { done = true; resolve(); } };
    try {
      if (win && typeof win.requestAnimationFrame === 'function') {
        win.requestAnimationFrame(finish);
      }
    } catch {}
    setTimeout(finish, fallbackMs);
  });
  // Apply print rules while measuring, and suppress screen-only decoration.
  const printMedia = css => css.replace(/@media\s+print\b/gi, '@media all')
    .replace(/@media\s+screen\b/gi, '@media not all');

  function snapshot(source, sectionClasses) {
    const copy = source.cloneNode(true);
    const originals = source.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select');
    copy.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select').forEach((control, index) => {
      const original = originals[index];
      if (['hidden','file','button','submit'].includes(original.type)) { control.remove(); return; }
      const value = document.createElement('span');
      value.className = 'emr-print-value ' + control.className;
      value.style.cssText = control.style.cssText;
      // Measurements need only their displayed value, rather than the empty
      // input's fixed width. Keep the label, value and unit on the same line.
      if (/^(?:p2|opd|ipd)_(?:temp|bp_sys|bp_dia|weight|height|pulse|resp|spo2)$/.test(original.id)) {
        value.classList.add('emr-print-number');
        const cell = control.closest('td');
        if (cell) cell.classList.add('emr-print-measurement');
      }
      if (original.type === 'checkbox' || original.type === 'radio') {
        value.textContent = original.checked ? '☑' : '☐';
        value.classList.add('emr-print-check');
        if (source.id === 'page2' && original.type === 'checkbox') {
          value.classList.add('emr-triage-check');
          value.textContent = original.checked ? '✓' : '';
          value.dataset.checked = String(original.checked);
        }
      } else {
        value.textContent = original.tagName === 'SELECT'
          ? (original.selectedOptions[0]?.textContent || '') : (original.value || '\u00a0');
        if (original.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') value.classList.add('emr-print-multiline');
      }
      control.replaceWith(value);
    });
    const canvases = source.querySelectorAll('canvas');
    copy.querySelectorAll('canvas').forEach((canvas,index) => {
      try { const img=document.createElement('img'); img.src=canvases[index].toDataURL(); img.className=canvas.className; img.width=canvas.width; img.height=canvas.height; canvas.replaceWith(img); } catch { canvas.remove(); }
    });
    copy.querySelectorAll('.no-print,.print-toolbar,button,script,iframe,object,embed,.emr-editor-history,.patient-summary-tab,.patient-summary-drawer,tr.print-empty-medication-row').forEach(node => node.remove());
    [copy,...copy.querySelectorAll('*')].forEach(node => {
      [...node.attributes].forEach(attr => { if (/^on/i.test(attr.name)) node.removeAttribute(attr.name); });
      node.removeAttribute('contenteditable');
    });
    copy.style.removeProperty('display'); copy.style.removeProperty('visibility');
    copy.style.setProperty('display',copy.id==='print-rx-layout'?'flex':'block','important');
    copy.style.setProperty('content-visibility','visible','important');
    copy.removeAttribute('hidden'); copy.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
    copy.classList.remove('emr-workflow-hidden','emr-permission-hidden');
    copy.classList.add('emr-print-section',...sectionClasses);
    return copy;
  }

  function withAncestors(source, copy) {
    // Preserve the report's scoped layout rules, without copying other tabs.
    let parent=source.parentElement;
    while(parent && parent.tagName!=='BODY' && parent.tagName!=='HTML') {
      const shell=parent.cloneNode(false);
      shell.removeAttribute('hidden'); shell.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
      shell.removeAttribute('style'); shell.style.setProperty('display','block','important'); shell.classList.add('emr-print-ancestor');
      // An inactive source page can have dimensions yet suppress all painting.
      shell.style.setProperty('content-visibility','visible','important');
      shell.classList.remove('no-print','emr-workflow-hidden','emr-permission-hidden');
      [...shell.attributes].forEach(attr=>{if(/^on/i.test(attr.name))shell.removeAttribute(attr.name);});
      shell.appendChild(copy); copy=shell; parent=parent.parentElement;
    }
    return copy;
  }

  async function stylesFor(sourceDocument, doc) {
    const assets=[];
    await Promise.all([...sourceDocument.querySelectorAll('style,link[rel="stylesheet"]')].map(async node => {
      // Preserve order while asynchronously loading same-origin CSS.
      const placeholder=doc.createElement('style'); doc.head.appendChild(placeholder);
      if(node.tagName==='STYLE') { placeholder.textContent=printMedia(node.textContent || ''); return; }
      const href = node.getAttribute('href');
      if (!href) return;
      try {
        const url=new URL(href,sourceDocument.baseURI || location.href);
        if(url.origin===location.origin) {
          try {
            const response=await fetch(url.href,{credentials:'same-origin'});
            if(response.ok) {
              let css=await response.text();
              css=css.replace(/url\((['"]?)(?!data:|#)([^)'"\s]+)\1\)/gi,(_,quote,path)=>`url("${new URL(path,url).href}")`);
              placeholder.textContent=printMedia(css);
            }
          } catch(fetchError) {
            console.warn('Could not load print layout:', url.pathname, fetchError);
          }
        } else {
          const link=doc.createElement('link');link.rel='stylesheet';link.href=url.href;
          assets.push(new Promise(resolve=>{link.onload=resolve;link.onerror=resolve;setTimeout(resolve,2000);}));placeholder.replaceWith(link);
        }
      } catch(urlError) {
        console.warn('Invalid stylesheet URL:', href, urlError);
      }
    }));
    await bounded(Promise.all(assets), 2000);
    // Resolve responsive rules once in the print frame. Native print preview
    // can use a different viewport, which must not reflow a measured sheet.
    const flattenMedia = rules => [...rules].map(rule => {
      // A paper sheet uses the desktop form structure. Mobile breakpoints
      // must not collapse its columns before the A4 fitting calculation.
      if (rule.type === 4 && /(?:min|max)-(?:width|height)\s*:/i.test(rule.conditionText)) return '';
      if (rule.type === 4) return doc.defaultView.matchMedia(rule.conditionText).matches
        ? flattenMedia(rule.cssRules) : '';
      return rule.cssText;
    }).join('\n');
    for (const sheet of [...doc.styleSheets]) {
      try {
        const css = flattenMedia(sheet.cssRules);
        if (sheet.ownerNode.tagName === 'STYLE') sheet.ownerNode.textContent = css;
        else { const style = doc.createElement('style'); style.textContent = css; sheet.ownerNode.replaceWith(style); }
      } catch { /* Keep cross-origin stylesheets when CSSOM access is restricted. */ }
    }
  }

  async function fitA4Sheets(doc, root, orientation, separatePages) {
    const sheetSelector = '.page-a4,.nursing-care-page,.paper-frame,.lab-print-page,.sheet-frame';
    const view = doc.defaultView;
    const visible = node => view.getComputedStyle(node).display !== 'none' && node.getClientRects().length;
    const reports = [];
    // Preserve the document's existing sheets (including nursing landscape
    // pages). Keep all ancestor classes/IDs for the original scoped styles.
    for (const report of [...root.children]) {
      const ipd = report.id === 'sub_ipd' ? report : report.querySelector('#sub_ipd');
      const soap = ipd?.querySelector('#ipd_soap_record');
      if (soap) {
        // v40.7: the IPD record is in S/O/A/P boxes. Keep the same two-page
        // balance as before: page 1 has S and O up to the respiratory
        // examination; page 2 has the rest of O, then A and P and the footer.
        const box = letter => soap.querySelector(`[data-soap-record-box="${letter}"]`);
        const clone = node => node.cloneNode(true);
        const oBox = box('o'), oBody = oBox?.querySelector('.emr-soap-body');
        const oChildren = oBody ? [...oBody.childNodes] : [];
        const examination = oChildren.find(node => node.nodeType === 1 && node.querySelector('[data-key="ipd_sec7"]'));
        if (examination && box('s') && box('a') && box('p')) {
          const examBody = examination.lastElementChild;
          const examNodes = [...examBody.childNodes];
          const examSplit = examNodes.indexOf(examBody.children[6]);
          const examPart = index => {
            if (examSplit <= 0) return index ? null : clone(examination);
            const half = clone(examination);
            half.lastElementChild.classList.add('emr-ipd-exam-body');
            half.lastElementChild.replaceChildren(...(index ? examNodes.slice(examSplit) : examNodes.slice(0, examSplit)).map(clone));
            return half;
          };
          const examAt = oChildren.indexOf(examination);
          const oPart = index => {
            const part = clone(oBox);
            part.querySelector('.emr-soap-body').replaceChildren(...(index
              ? [examPart(1), ...oChildren.slice(examAt + 1).map(clone)]
              : [...oChildren.slice(0, examAt).map(clone), examPart(0)]).filter(Boolean));
            return part;
          };
          const soapPage = boxes => { const shell = soap.cloneNode(false); shell.append(...boxes); return shell; };
          const children = [...ipd.childNodes];
          const soapAt = children.indexOf(soap);
          const title = ipd.querySelector('[data-key="ipd_title"]');
          const pages = [
            [...children.slice(0, soapAt).map(clone), soapPage([clone(box('s')), oPart(0)])],
            [title && clone(title), soapPage([oPart(1), clone(box('a')), clone(box('p'))]), ...children.slice(soapAt + 1).map(clone)].filter(Boolean),
          ];
          for (const [index, nodes] of pages.entries()) {
            const copy = report.cloneNode(true);
            const form = copy.id === 'sub_ipd' ? copy : copy.querySelector('#sub_ipd');
            form.replaceChildren(...nodes);
            form.classList.add('emr-ipd-print-page');
            form.querySelector('#daily_monitoring_feed')?.remove();
            form.querySelector('[data-key="ipd_title"]')?.append(doc.createTextNode(` · ${index+1}/2`));
            reports.push({content:copy,orientation});
          }
          continue;
        }
      }
      if (ipd) {
        // Balance history and examination across two A4 pages in the isolated
        // print copy. The editable web form is never divided or changed.
        const record = ipd.querySelector('.paraclinic-medical-main') || ipd;
        const sections = [...record.children].filter(node => node.classList.contains('opd-section'));
        const splitAt = [...record.childNodes].indexOf(sections[6]);
        if (splitAt > 0) {
          const children = [...record.childNodes];
          const heading = record.firstElementChild;
          const examination = sections[6];
          const examBody = examination.lastElementChild;
          // Continue the system examination on page two, after the respiratory examination.
          // This balances the two pages and avoids shrinking all of page two
          // just to keep the entire examination on a single sheet.
          const examSplit = [...(examBody?.childNodes || [])].indexOf(examBody?.children[6]);
          const examHalves = examSplit > 0 ? [0,1].map(index => {
            const half = examination.cloneNode(true);
            const nodes = [...examBody.childNodes];
            half.lastElementChild.classList.add('emr-ipd-exam-body');
            half.lastElementChild.replaceChildren(...(index ? nodes.slice(examSplit) : nodes.slice(0,examSplit)).map(node=>node.cloneNode(true)));
            return half;
          }) : null;
          for (const [index,nodes] of [children.slice(0,splitAt),children.slice(splitAt)].entries()) {
            const copy = report.cloneNode(true);
            const form = copy.id === 'sub_ipd' ? copy : copy.querySelector('#sub_ipd');
            const formBody = form.querySelector('.paraclinic-medical-main') || form;
            formBody.replaceChildren(...nodes.map(node => node.cloneNode(true)));
            form.classList.add('emr-ipd-print-page');
            form.querySelector('#daily_monitoring_feed')?.remove();
            if (examHalves) {
              if (index) formBody.querySelector('.opd-section')?.replaceWith(examHalves[1]);
              else formBody.append(examHalves[0]);
            }
            if (index && heading) formBody.prepend(heading.cloneNode(true));
            const pageHeading=formBody.firstElementChild;
            if (pageHeading) pageHeading.append(doc.createTextNode(` · ${index+1}/2`));
            reports.push({content:copy,orientation});
          }
          continue;
        }
      }
      const pages = [...report.querySelectorAll(sheetSelector)].filter(node =>
        visible(node) && !node.parentElement.closest(sheetSelector));
      if (!pages.length) { reports.push({content:report, orientation}); continue; }
      for (const page of pages) {
        const pageOrientation = page.matches('.nursing-care-page') ? 'landscape' : orientation;
        let content = page.cloneNode(true), ancestor = page.parentElement;
        // Paper wrappers must expand with the fitted layout, rather than
        // retaining their old millimetre cap after the text is scaled.
        content.style.setProperty('width', '100%', 'important');
        content.style.setProperty('max-width', 'none', 'important');
        content.style.setProperty('min-width', '0', 'important');
        while (ancestor && ancestor !== root) {
          const shell = ancestor.cloneNode(false);
          shell.classList.add('emr-print-ancestor');
          shell.appendChild(content); content = shell; ancestor = ancestor.parentElement;
        }
        reports.push({content, orientation:pageOrientation});
      }
    }
    if (!separatePages && reports.every(report => report.orientation === orientation)) {
      const group = doc.createElement('div');
      reports.forEach(report => group.appendChild(report.content));
      reports.splice(0, reports.length, {content:group, orientation});
    }
    root.replaceChildren();
    const style = doc.createElement('style');
    style.textContent = `
      .emr-ipd-print-page .opd-section {padding:6px!important;margin-bottom:8px!important;box-shadow:none!important}
      .emr-ipd-print-page .emr-ipd-exam-body {line-height:1.5!important}
      @page emr-a4-portrait {size:A4 portrait;margin:10mm}
      @page emr-a4-landscape {size:A4 landscape;margin:10mm}
      html {height:auto!important;min-height:0!important;zoom:1!important}
      html body.emr-report-print {height:auto!important;zoom:1!important}
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-sheet {
        display:block!important;position:relative!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;
        margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;max-width:none!important;
        contain:size layout paint!important;overflow:hidden!important;break-inside:avoid!important;page-break-inside:avoid!important;
        break-before:auto!important;page-break-before:auto!important;
        break-after:page!important;page-break-after:always!important;
      }
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-sheet:last-child {break-after:auto!important;page-break-after:auto!important}
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-content {
        display:flow-root!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;
        max-width:none!important;transform-origin:top left!important;page:inherit!important;
      }
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-content * {
        break-before:auto!important;page-break-before:auto!important;
        break-after:auto!important;page-break-after:auto!important;page:inherit!important;
      }
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-content .sheet-frame {height:auto!important;transform:none!important}
      html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-content .paper-frame {overflow:visible!important}
      @media print {html body.emr-report-print {width:auto!important} html body.emr-report-print .emr-a4-sheet {width:100%!important} html body.emr-report-print .emr-print-root {overflow:hidden!important}}
    `;
    doc.head.appendChild(style);
    for (const report of reports) {
      const landscape = report.orientation === 'landscape';
      const width = (landscape ? 277 : 190) * 96 / 25.4;
      const height = (landscape ? 190 : 277) * 96 / 25.4;
      const sheet = doc.createElement('div'); sheet.className = 'emr-a4-sheet';
      sheet.style.setProperty('width', width + 'px', 'important');
      sheet.style.setProperty('page', 'emr-a4-' + report.orientation, 'important');
      const content = doc.createElement('div'); content.className = 'emr-a4-content';
      content.style.setProperty('width', width + 'px', 'important');
      content.appendChild(report.content);
      // Inline priority also overrides legacy #ID print rules. Pagination now
      // belongs to the sheet wrapper, rather than individual form elements.
      for (const node of [content, ...content.querySelectorAll('*')]) {
        for (const property of ['break-before','break-after','page-break-before','page-break-after']) {
          node.style.setProperty(property, 'auto', 'important');
        }
        node.style.setProperty('page', 'inherit', 'important');
      }
      sheet.appendChild(content); root.appendChild(sheet);
      // Force font selection after cloning each sheet, then wait for those
      // fonts before measuring. Hidden source pages may not have loaded them.
      content.getBoundingClientRect();
      await bounded(doc.fonts?.ready);
      await bounded(Promise.all([...content.querySelectorAll('img')].map(img => img.decode?.().catch(() => {}))));
      await nextFrame(view);
      resizeA4Sheet(doc, sheet, width, height);
    }
  }

  function alignTriageCheckRows(content) {
    const triage=content.querySelector('#page2');
    if (!triage) return;
    const rows=[...triage.querySelectorAll('.symptom-row')];
    rows.forEach(row=>row.style.removeProperty('min-height'));
    // Paired checklist columns keep the same row boundaries after text wraps
    // at the final A4 width. This operates only on the isolated print copy.
    for (const line of triage.querySelectorAll('tr')) {
      const columns=[...line.children].filter(cell=>cell.tagName==='TD')
        .map(cell=>[...cell.children].filter(row=>row.classList.contains('symptom-row')))
        .filter(column=>column.length);
      if (columns.length!==2) continue;
      for (let index=0;index<Math.min(columns[0].length,columns[1].length);index++) {
        const pair=columns.map(column=>column[index]);
        const height=Math.max(...pair.map(row=>row.offsetHeight));
        pair.forEach(row=>row.style.setProperty('min-height',height+'px','important'));
      }
    }
  }

  function resizeA4Sheet(doc, sheet, width, height) {
    const content = sheet.querySelector('.emr-a4-content');
    const visible = node => doc.defaultView.getComputedStyle(node).display !== 'none' && node.getClientRects().length;
    // Leave room for native print headers, footers and printer rounding.
    const availableWidth = width - 2, availableHeight = height - 10 * 96 / 25.4;
    content.style.setProperty('position', 'absolute', 'important');
    content.style.setProperty('top', '0', 'important');
    content.style.setProperty('left', '0', 'important');
    content.style.setProperty('transform', 'none', 'important');

    function layout(scale) {
      // Compensate the layout width for the text scale. The final physical form keeps
      // the full paper width, instead of becoming a narrow column on the left.
      content.style.setProperty('width', (availableWidth / scale) + 'px', 'important');
      content.style.setProperty('zoom', '1', 'important');
      content.style.setProperty('transform', `scale(${scale})`, 'important');
      alignTriageCheckRows(content);
      const box = content.getBoundingClientRect();
      let right = box.right, bottom = box.bottom;
      for (const node of content.querySelectorAll('*')) {
        if (!visible(node)) continue;
        const bounds = node.getBoundingClientRect();
        right = Math.max(right, bounds.right); bottom = Math.max(bottom, bounds.bottom);
      }
      return {width:right - box.left, height:bottom - box.top};
    }
    const fits = bounds => bounds.width <= availableWidth + .5 && bounds.height <= availableHeight;
    let scale = 1, bounds = layout(scale);
    if (!fits(bounds)) {
      let upper = 1, lower = .5;
      while (lower > .01 && !fits(layout(lower))) { upper = lower; lower /= 2; }
      // Find the largest readable text scale which fits both dimensions.
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 14; attempt += 1) {
        const middle = (lower + upper) / 2;
        if (fits(layout(middle))) lower = middle; else upper = middle;
      }
      scale = lower;
      bounds = layout(scale);
    }
    sheet.dataset.fitScale = String(scale);
    sheet.style.setProperty('height', Math.ceil(bounds.height) + 'px', 'important');
  }


  window.printEmrReport = async ({selectors,classes=[],sectionClasses=[],title=document.title,sourceDocument=document,orientation='portrait',separatePages=true}) => {
    window.logRecordAccess?.('print', title); // v41.7 record view log
    if(activePrint && !activePrint.invoked) return activePrint.ready;
    // A new click after closing the browser preview can start another report,
    // even in browsers which do not dispatch afterprint consistently.
    activePrint?.cleanup();
    const sources=selectors.map(selector=>sourceDocument.querySelector(selector)).filter(Boolean);
    if(!sources.length) { window.alert('The report is not ready to print. / មិនមានទម្រង់សម្រាប់បោះពុម្ព។'); return; }
    let positioningReady=Promise.resolve();
    if(!document.querySelector('link[data-report-print-style]')) {
      const link=document.createElement('link');link.rel='stylesheet';link.href='/public/report-print.css?v=3670';link.dataset.reportPrintStyle='';
      positioningReady=new Promise(resolve=>{link.onload=resolve;link.onerror=resolve;setTimeout(resolve,1500);});
      document.head.appendChild(link);
    }
    const previousFocus=document.activeElement;
    const frame=document.createElement('iframe');frame.title=title;frame.className='emr-report-print-frame';frame.dataset.orientation=orientation;frame.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');frame.tabIndex=-1;
    const job={ready:null,invoked:false,cleanup:()=>{
      frame.remove();
      if(activePrint===job){activePrint=null;previousFocus?.focus({preventScroll:true});}
    }};
    activePrint=job;
    // The authenticated blank page permits report styles inside its own CSP;
    // the application's stricter CSP and original form stay untouched.
    const loaded=new Promise(resolve=>{
      let done=false;
      const finish=()=>{if(!done){done=true;resolve();}};
      frame.onload=finish;
      frame.onerror=finish;
      setTimeout(finish,2500);
    });
    frame.src='/print-preview';
    job.ready=(async()=>{
      try {
        // Position the rendering frame off screen before attaching it. It must
        // remain rendered (not display:none) for every browser to paginate it.
        await positioningReady;
        document.body.appendChild(frame);
        await loaded;if(activePrint!==job)return;
        const doc=frame.contentDocument||frame.contentWindow?.document;
        if(!doc||!doc.body) throw new Error('Could not open print preview frame.');
        doc.head.replaceChildren();doc.body.replaceChildren();doc.documentElement.lang=sourceDocument.documentElement.lang||'km';
        const meta=doc.createElement('meta');meta.setAttribute('charset','utf-8');doc.head.appendChild(meta);
        const base=doc.createElement('base');base.href=sourceDocument.baseURI||location.href;doc.head.appendChild(base);doc.title=title;
        // BODY sources (embedded forms and generated invoices) become DIV reports.
        doc.body.className=['emr-report-print',sourceDocument.body.className,...classes].join(' ');
        const root=doc.createElement('main');root.className='emr-print-root';root.style.setProperty('display','block','important');
        sources.forEach(source=>{
          let copy=snapshot(source,sectionClasses);
          if(copy.tagName==='BODY'){const div=doc.createElement('div');div.className=copy.className;while(copy.firstChild)div.appendChild(copy.firstChild);copy=div;}
          root.appendChild(doc.importNode(withAncestors(source,copy),true));
        });
        doc.body.appendChild(root);
        await stylesFor(sourceDocument,doc);
        const style=doc.createElement('style');style.textContent=`
          @font-face {font-family:Moul;src:url('/public/fonts/Moul-Khmer.woff2') format('woff2');unicode-range:U+1780-17FF,U+19E0-19FF,U+200C-200D,U+25CC}
          @font-face {font-family:Moul;src:url('/public/fonts/Moul-Latin.woff2') format('woff2');unicode-range:U+0000-00FF}
          @font-face {font-family:'Khmer OS Battambang';src:url('/public/fonts/Battambang-Regular.ttf') format('truetype')}
          @font-face {font-family:EMRPrintBattambang;src:url('/public/fonts/Battambang-Regular.ttf') format('truetype')}
          html {background:#e5e7eb!important}
          *,*::before,*::after {animation:none!important;transition:none!important}
          html body.emr-report-print {box-sizing:border-box!important;width:${orientation==='landscape'?'297':'210'}mm!important;max-width:100%!important;margin:12px auto!important;padding:10mm!important;background:white!important;color:black!important;min-height:${orientation==='landscape'?'210':'297'}mm;font-family:EMRPrintBattambang,Arial,sans-serif!important}
          html body.emr-report-print .emr-print-root,html body.emr-report-print .emr-print-ancestor {display:block!important;width:100%!important;max-width:none!important;height:auto!important;min-height:0!important;max-height:none!important;overflow:visible!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;break-after:auto!important;break-before:auto!important}
          html body.emr-report-print .emr-print-section {display:block!important;width:100%!important;max-width:none!important;height:auto!important;max-height:none!important;overflow:visible!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;break-after:auto!important;page-break-after:auto!important}
          html body.emr-report-print #print-rx-layout.emr-print-section {display:flex!important}
          .emr-print-root > :not(:first-child) {break-before:${separatePages?'page':'auto'}}
          .emr-print-value {box-sizing:border-box!important;max-width:100%;display:inline-block;min-height:1em;white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:normal;word-break:normal;color:black;vertical-align:baseline}
          .emr-print-multiline {display:block;width:100%;height:auto!important;min-height:1em!important}
          .emr-print-number {font-size:inherit!important;line-height:inherit!important;width:auto!important;min-width:1.5em!important;white-space:nowrap!important;overflow-wrap:normal!important;word-break:normal!important;text-align:center}
          .emr-print-measurement {white-space:nowrap!important}
          .emr-print-check {width:auto!important;min-width:0!important}
          #page2 .emr-triage-check {display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center;justify-content:center;box-sizing:border-box!important;width:12px!important;min-width:12px!important;height:12px!important;min-height:12px!important;padding:0!important;border:1px solid #222;border-radius:2px;font:10px/1 Arial,sans-serif!important;vertical-align:middle;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0}
          #page2 .symptom-row {display:grid!important;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 12px!important;column-gap:8px!important;align-items:start!important;box-sizing:border-box!important}
          #page2 .symptom-text {min-width:0;overflow-wrap:normal;word-break:normal}
          #page2 .symptom-row>.emr-triage-check {margin:2px 0 0!important;align-self:start}
          .no-print,.print-toolbar,.emr-editor-history {display:none!important}
          .emr-permission-readonly::before {display:none!important}
          @media print {html {background:white!important} html body.emr-report-print {width:auto!important;max-width:none!important;min-height:0!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important} @page {size:A4 ${orientation==='landscape'?'landscape':'portrait'};margin:10mm}}
        `;doc.head.appendChild(style);
        doc.body.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (doc.fonts?.load) await bounded(Promise.all([
          doc.fonts.load('14px EMRPrintBattambang'),
          doc.fonts.load('14px "Khmer OS Battambang"'),
          doc.fonts.load('14px Moul', 'ភាសាខ្មែរ'),
          doc.fonts.load('14px Moul', 'Hospital'),
        ]));
        await bounded(doc.fonts?.ready);await bounded(Promise.all([...doc.images].map(img=>img.decode?.().catch(()=>{}))));
        // Fit each existing report sheet in both dimensions. A fixed sheet box contains
        // the scaled form so long sheets do not leave a second page behind. Only this isolated print snapshot is changed.
        await fitA4Sheets(doc, root, orientation, separatePages);
        await nextFrame(frame.contentWindow);
        if(activePrint!==job)return;
        // Only the isolated report is passed to native Print. Keep the snapshot
        // alive until preview closes; do not clear it on a short timer.
        frame.contentWindow.addEventListener('afterprint',job.cleanup,{once:true});
        frame.dataset.ready='true';job.invoked=true;
        frame.contentWindow.focus();frame.contentWindow.print();
      } catch(error) {job.cleanup();console.error('Print preview error:',error);window.alert('Could not open print preview. Please try again. / មិនអាចបើកការមើលមុនបោះពុម្ពបានទេ។ សូមព្យាយាមម្តងទៀត។');}
    })();
    return job.ready;
  };

  window.previewEmrHtml = async (html,title,options={}) => {
    const sourceDocument=new DOMParser().parseFromString(html,'text/html');
    // Preserve organization names in generated cashier documents without executing scripts.
    let branding=window.organizationBranding;
    if(!branding)try{const response=await fetch('/api/organization/branding',{credentials:'same-origin'});if(response.ok)branding=await response.json();}catch{}
    if(branding){
      const names=[[sourceDocument.querySelector('[data-organization-name="kh"]')?.textContent||'មន្ទីរពេទ្យបង្អែក បរកែវ',branding.nameKh],[sourceDocument.querySelector('[data-organization-name="en"]')?.textContent||'Borkeo Referral Hospital',branding.nameEn]];
      const walker=sourceDocument.createTreeWalker(sourceDocument.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
      while(walker.nextNode())for(const [oldName,newName] of names)if(newName)walker.currentNode.nodeValue=walker.currentNode.nodeValue.split(oldName).join(newName);
    }
    return window.printEmrReport({sourceDocument,selectors:options.selectors||['body'],separatePages:options.separatePages??true,title});
  };
})();

// Read the release label from the package; retain the incomplete-folder launcher fallback.
const APP_VERSION = (() => {
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(projectRoot,'package.json'),'utf8')).version.match(/^\d+\.\d+/)?.[0] || '22.2'; }
  catch { return '22.2'; }
})();

const clinicalFormTypes = new Set(CLINICAL_FORM_TYPES);
const clinicalWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormReadRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR', 'DIRECTOR', 'REGISTRATION', 'TRIAGE', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD', 'NURSE', 'MIDWIFE']);
const clinicalFormWriteRoles = new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR', 'DOCTOR','CHIEF_WARD']);

function landingPathForRole(role, wardCode = '') {
  role = String(role || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','VICE_DIRECTOR'].includes(role)) return '/billing';
  if (role === 'LAB') return '/lab';
  if (role === 'IMAGING') return '/imaging';
  if (role === 'TRIAGE') return '/triage';
  if (role === 'REGISTRATION') return '/emr?view=register';
  if (role === 'NURSE' && String(wardCode || '').toLowerCase() === 'opd') return '/opd-paraclinic-nurse';
  if (role === 'WARD_HEAD' || role === 'CHIEF_WARD') return '/ward-medicines';
  if (role === 'NURSE' || role === 'MIDWIFE') return '/departments';
  return '/home';
}

function messagingVisibility(session) {
  return { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session), username: session.username };
}

function messagingRoleAllowed(role) {
  return new Set(['ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','REGISTRATION','TRIAGE','DOCTOR','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CHIEF_WARD','WARD_HEAD','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT','LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY']).has(String(role || '').toUpperCase());
}

function destinationExists(type, code='') {
  type = String(type || '').toUpperCase();
  if (['LAB','IMAGING','PHARMACY','ADMIN','VICE_DIRECTOR','DIRECTOR','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(type)) return true;
  if (type !== 'WARD') return false;
  return WARDS.some(ward => ward.code === String(code || '').toLowerCase());
}

function automaticWardMessage(database, actor, patient, subject, body, options = {}) {
  const wardCode = String(options.wardCode || patientWorkWard(patient) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!wardCode) return null;
  return createSystemMessage(database, {
    senderUserId: actor.userId,
    senderRole: actor.role,
    senderWardCode: actor.wardCode || '',
    destinationType: 'WARD',
    destinationCode: wardCode,
    destinationRole: options.destinationRole || '',
    subject,
    body,
    patientKey: patient.patient_key,
  });
}

function notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted(database, session, patient, bill, payments = []) {
  if (String(patient?.service_mode || '').toLowerCase() !== 'opd') return null;
  const money = (amount, currency) => currency === 'USD'
    ? `$${Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`
    : `${Math.round(Number(amount || 0)).toLocaleString('en-US')} ៛`;
  const paid = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.paid || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ') || payments.map(item => money(item.amount, item.currency)).join(' + ');
  const waived = ['KHR','USD'].map(currency => {
    const amount = Number(bill?.summary?.[currency]?.waived || 0);
    return amount > 0 ? money(amount, currency) : '';
  }).filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
  const settlement = paid || (waived ? `Fully waived: ${waived}` : 'Invoice settled');
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const invoice = bill?.invoice?.invoiceNo || bill?.invoice?.id || '—';
  return automaticWardMessage(database,
    { userId:session.user_id, role:sessionRole(session), wardCode:sessionWard(session) },
    patient,
    'Cashier payment completed',
    `${name} · invoice ${invoice} · ${settlement}. The OPD invoice is settled; the patient may continue with the OPD doctor.`,
    { wardCode:'opd', destinationRole:'DOCTOR' });
}

function admissionWardCode(update) {
  const room = String(update?.roomCode || '').toLowerCase();
  const department = String(update?.departmentCode || '').toLowerCase();
  if (room === 'medical') return 'medicine';
  if (room === 'surgical') return 'surgery';
  if (['emergency','pediatric','gynecology','obstetric','tb','infectious','icu','ncu'].includes(room)) return room;
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') return room === 'obstetric' ? 'obstetric' : 'gynecology';
  return department;
}

function notifyTriageReferral(database, session, patient) {
  const department = String(patient.department_code || '').toLowerCase();
  const name = patient.full_name || patient.case_number || patient.patient_key;
  const actor = { userId: session.user_id, role: sessionRole(session), wardCode: sessionWard(session) };
  const getWardKh = (code) => {
    try {
      const w = database.findWard?.(code);
      if (w?.nameKh) return w.nameKh;
    } catch (_) {}
    const map = {
      medicine: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺទូទៅ',
      surgery: 'ផ្នែកវះកាត់',
      pediatric: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      pediatrics: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺកុមារ',
      maternity: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      obstetric: 'ផ្នែកសម្ភព',
      gynecology: 'ផ្នែករោគស្ត្រី',
      emergency: 'ផ្នែកសង្គ្រោះបន្ទាន់',
      tb: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺរបេង',
      infectious: 'ផ្នែកជំងឺឆ្លង',
      dental: 'ផ្នែកធ្មេញ',
      mental: 'ផ្នែកសុខភាពផ្លូវចិត្ត',
      opd: 'ផ្នែកពិគ្រោះក្រៅ'
    };
    return map[code] || (code ? `ផ្នែក ${code}` : 'ផ្នែកសម្រាកពេទ្យ');
  };
  const send = (wardCode, destinationRole) => {
    const targetDept = getWardKh(wardCode);
    const msg = `អ្នកជំងឺឈ្មោះ ${name} ត្រូវបានបញ្ជូនពីផ្នែកទ្រីយ៉ាមកផ្នែក ${targetDept.replace(/^ផ្នែក\s*/, '')} សូមពិនិត្យមើល និងរៀបចំជូនអ្នកជំងឺ`;
    return automaticWardMessage(
      database, actor, patient,
      msg,
      msg,
      { wardCode, destinationRole },
    );
  };
  if (department === 'gyneco-obstetric') {
    send('gynecology', 'NURSE');
    send('obstetric', 'MIDWIFE');
    return;
  }
  const wardCode = patientWorkWard(patient) || department;
  if (!wardCode || wardCode === 'opd') return;
  send(wardCode, wardCode === 'obstetric' ? 'MIDWIFE' : 'NURSE');
}

// v42.4: behind a hosting proxy (TRUST_PROXY=true) the client address is the
// last address in X-Forwarded-For (added by the proxy itself; earlier entries
// can be sent by the client). Login lockout, the audit log and the record view
// log all use this address. Without TRUST_PROXY the header is ignored.
function requestIp(request) {
  if (request.borkeoTrustProxy) {
    const forwarded = String(request.headers['x-forwarded-for'] || '').split(',').map(part => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (forwarded.length) return forwarded[forwarded.length - 1];
  }
  return request.socket.remoteAddress ?? 'unknown';
}

const htmlTemplateCache = new Map();
const staticFileCache = new Map();
const staticCompressedCache = new Map();

function compressIfNeeded(request, response, buffer, contentType) {
  if (!buffer || buffer.length < 512) return buffer;
  const isCompressible = /^(text\/|application\/json|application\/javascript|font\/ttf|image\/svg\+xml)/i.test(contentType);
  if (!isCompressible) return buffer;

  const accept = String(request?.headers?.['accept-encoding'] || '');
  if (/\bgzip\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'gzip');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.gzipSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  if (/\bdeflate\b/i.test(accept)) {
    response.setHeader('Content-Encoding', 'deflate');
    response.setHeader('Vary', 'Accept-Encoding');
    return zlib.deflateSync(buffer, { level: 6 });
  }
  return buffer;
}

function getProcessedHtml(filePath, contentType, security, APP_VERSION) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
  const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${stat.mtimeMs}:${APP_VERSION}:${security?.requestPath || ''}`;
  const cached = htmlTemplateCache.get(filePath);
  if (cached && cached.cacheKey === cacheKey) {
    return cached.bytes;
  }
  let html = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  html = html.replace(/(<span\b[^>]*\bdata-app-version\b[^>]*>)[^<]*(<\/span>)/g, (_, open, close) => `${open}v${APP_VERSION}${close}`);
  html = html.replace(/Borkeo EMR v\d+\.\d+/g, `Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION}`);
  html = html.replace(/<head\b[^>]*>/i, head => head + '<style>.emr-language-switch, [data-emr-language], #langBtn, .lang-btn, body #emr-site-banner .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, #emr-session-control .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch, .portal-account-actions .emr-language-switch { display: none !important; visibility: hidden !important; opacity: 0 !important; pointer-events: none !important; width: 0 !important; height: 0 !important; max-width: 0 !important; max-height: 0 !important; margin: 0 !important; padding: 0 !important; border: 0 !important; position: absolute !important; left: -9999px !important; clip: rect(0,0,0,0) !important; }</style><script src="/public/emr-language-lock.js?v=3760"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-required-fields.css?v=3770"><script src="/public/emr-required-fields.js?v=3770"></script>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/design-system.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/components.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/app-shell.css?v=3980"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-banner.css?v=4100"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/soap-note.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-data.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/worklists-feedback.css?v=3970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/interface-theme.css?v=1970"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/npca.css?v=4280"><script src="/public/npca-widget.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/npca-forms.js?v=4280" defer></script><script src="/public/components.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/nav-config.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/app-shell.js?v=3980" defer></script><script src="/public/patient-banner.js?v=4170" defer></script><script src="/public/soap-note.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/clinical-data.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/worklists-feedback.js?v=3970" defer></script><script src="/public/interface-theme.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/shared-controls.css?v=1970"><script src="/public/request-badges.js?v=1970" defer></script></head>');
  // v42.3: one medication unit table for every page (old Khmer unit labels shown as mg, mL, mcg, g, IU).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/record-access.js?v=4570" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medicine-units.js?v=4230"></script></head>');
  // v42.5: one medication route table for every page (new lines: IV, IM, SC and the simple routes).
  html = html.replace('</head>', '<script src="/public/medication-routes.js?v=4250"></script></head>');
  // v42.3: medicine name search in the prescription forms (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medicine-autocomplete.css?v=4250"><script src="/public/medicine-autocomplete.js?v=4250" defer></script><script src="/public/medicine-dose-check.js?v=4250" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/maternal.css?v=4260"><script src="/public/maternal-emr.js?v=4260" defer></script></head>');
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-encounter-editor.css?v=3800"><script src="/public/emr-encounter-editor.js?v=3800" defer></script></head>');
  // v41.0: structured allergy record, problem list and nursing demographics (medical record page only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/clinical-summary.css?v=4100"><script src="/public/clinical-summary.js?v=4100" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/display-rules.css?v=4110"><script src="/public/display-rules.js?v=4110" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/medication-safety.css?v=4120"><script src="/public/medication-safety.js?v=4120" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/periop-forms.css?v=4160"><script src="/public/periop-forms.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/fluid-balance.js?v=4160" defer></script><script src="/public/early-warning.js?v=4160" defer></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/patient-documents.css?v=4170"><script src="/public/patient-documents.js?v=4170" defer></script></head>');
  // v43.3: modern clinical consultation workspace and integrated patient record (EMR.html only).
  if(path.basename(filePath)==='EMR.html')html=html.replace('</head>','<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.css?v=4330"><script src="/public/emr-consultation-workspace.js?v=4330" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-shared-encounter.js?v=4700" defer></script></head>');
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-version" content="${APP_VERSION}"></head>`);
  // New text without approved Khmer is highlighted unless SHOW_UNTRANSLATED=0 (public/new-text.js).
  const showUntranslated = globalThis.process?.env?.SHOW_UNTRANSLATED === '0' ? '0' : '1';
  html = html.replace('</head>', `<meta name="borkeo-show-untranslated" content="${showUntranslated}"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/new-text.css?v=4060"><script src="/public/new-text-data.js?v=4060"></script><script src="/public/new-text.js?v=4060"></script></head>`);
  if (!html.includes('/public/emr-language.js')) {
    const langVersion = APP_VERSION.split('.').slice(0, 2).join('') + '0';
    const languageAssets = `<link rel="stylesheet" href="/public/emr-language.css?v=${langVersion}"><script src="/public/emr-language-data.js?v=1750" defer></script><script src="/public/emr-language.js?v=${langVersion}" defer></script>`;
    html = html.replace('</head>', `${languageAssets}</head>`);
  }
  html = html.replace(/(\/public\/[^"'\s<>]+\?v=[^"'\s<>]+)/g, asset => `${asset}&build=${APP_VERSION}`);
  if (path.basename(filePath) === 'stock.html') {
    const reqPath = String(security?.requestPath || '');
    if (reqPath.startsWith('/lab')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="imaging-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
      html = html.replace(/<link[^>]*imaging\.css[^>]*>/g, '');
    } else if (reqPath.startsWith('/imaging')) {
      html = html.replace(/<section class="lab-toolbar[\s\S]*?<\/section>/g, '');
