        paymentStatusForPatient, encounterAccess,
        messagingRoleAllowed, destinationExists, safeAttachmentName,
        orderActorAllowed, labWardWorkflowRole, opdConsultationPaymentRecorded,
        syncOpdParaclinicToRecord, addCatalogCharge, addParaclinicCharges, automaticWardMessage,
        hefLookup, nssfLookup, notifyOpdDoctorsPaymentCompleted, updateWorkflowStageAfterPayment,
        badRequest, recordData, calendarDate, sanitizePatientSummary,
        patientDataVisibleForRole, validateDailyMonitoringDates,
        buildIpdDischargeSummary, sanitizeAdmissionAction,
        mergeRecordDataForRole, mergePatientSummaryForRole,
        clinicalWriteRoles, clinicalFormReadRoles,
        admissionWardCode, notifyTriageReferral, releasePatientFromHospitalMap,
        nextRegistrationQueueNumber, ensureUniqueRegistrationQueueNumber,
        registrationQueueNumberFromData, wardMedicineOrderDates, patientWorkWard,
        diagnosisSearch, followupDatePolicy, hospitalStateForSession,
        sanitizeHospitalState, mergeHospitalStateForSession,
        clinicalFormTypes, clinicalFormWriteRoles, clinicalRecordResponse,
        sanitizePatientSnapshot, sanitizeClinicalFormData,
        pmrsPaymentCategoryStatus, paidOpdQueuePatients,
      };
      if (await router.handle(ctx)) return;

      if (config.demoMode && pathname === '/pmrs-import') {
        return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'PMRS2 transfer is unavailable in the username-only demo.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }

      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/') {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }


      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/health') {
        return sendJson(response, 200, { status: 'ok' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/login') {
        // An account that must change its password stays on /login, where the
        // change-password form is shown; redirecting it would loop (v39.9).
        if (session && session.must_change_password !== 1) {
          const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
          return redirect(response, identity.needsContext ? '/context' : landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, 'login.html'), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && publicFiles.has(pathname)) {
        const [filename, contentType] = publicFiles.get(pathname);
        const isStaticFontOrImage = contentType.startsWith('font/') || contentType.startsWith('image/');
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.publicDirectory, filename), contentType, {
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, isStaticFontOrImage);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && (pathname.startsWith('/data/gazetteer/') || pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/'))) {
        const prefix = pathname.startsWith('/public/data/gazetteer/') ? '/public/data/gazetteer/' : '/data/gazetteer/';
        const relative = pathname.slice(prefix.length);
        if (!relative || relative.includes('..')) {
          return sendJson(response, 400, { error: 'Invalid path.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const filePath = path.join(projectRoot, 'data', 'gazetteer', relative);
        if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
          const contentType = relative.endsWith('.json') ? 'application/json; charset=utf-8' : 'text/plain; charset=utf-8';
          return sendFile(response, filePath, contentType, {
            secure: config.cookieSecure,
          }, true);
        }
        return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Gazetteer data not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
      }

      if (request.method === 'GET' && protectedPages.has(pathname)) {
        if (!session) return redirect(response, '/login', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const identity = buildIdentity(database, session);
        if (identity.needsContext && pathname !== '/context') {
          return redirect(response, '/context', { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/home' && ['LAB','IMAGING','TRIAGE','REGISTRATION','NURSE','MIDWIFE','CLERK','ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          return redirect(response, landingPathForRole(identity.activeRole, identity.activeWard?.code), { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && ['CLERK', 'ACCOUNTANT'].includes(String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase())) {
          const patientKey = url.searchParams.get('patient') || url.searchParams.get('patientKey') || '';
          const target = patientKey ? `/billing?patientKey=${encodeURIComponent(patientKey)}&history=1` : '/billing';
          return redirect(response, target, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/emr' && String(identity.activeRole || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRIAGE') {
          const requestedView = url.searchParams.get('view');
          if (requestedView !== 'triage') {
            url.searchParams.set('view', 'triage');
            return redirect(response, `${url.pathname}?${url.searchParams.toString()}`, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
          }
        }
        if(pathname==='/emr' && url.searchParams.get('patient') && !RECORD_MANAGEMENT.includes(identity.activeRole)){
          const row=database.findPatient(url.searchParams.get('patient'));
          if(row){
            const policy=encounterAccess(database,session,row);
            if(!policy.canView)return sendJson(response,403,{error:'អ្នកមិនមានសិទ្ធិមើលកំណត់ត្រានេះទេ។'},{secure:config.cookieSecure});
            const requested=url.searchParams.get('view')||'';
            const allowed=requested===policy.view || policy.view==='medical'&&['daily','discharge'].includes(requested) || policy.view==='opd'&&requested==='opd-paraclinic';
            if(!allowed){url.searchParams.set('view',policy.view);return redirect(response,url.pathname+'?'+url.searchParams.toString(),{secure:config.cookieSecure});}
          }
        }
        if (pathname !== '/context' && !roleCanPage(identity.activeRole, pathname)) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'Your active role cannot open this page.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/ipd-transfer' && (identity.activeRole !== 'DOCTOR' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The IPD transfer workspace is available to OPD doctors only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        if (pathname === '/opd-paraclinic-nurse' && (identity.activeRole !== 'NURSE' || identity.activeWard?.code !== 'opd')) {
          return sendJson(response, 403, { error: 'The OPD paraclinic handoff queue is available to OPD nurses only.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        }
        const [filename, legacy] = protectedPages.get(pathname);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, filename), 'text/html; charset=utf-8', {
          legacy,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
          requestPath: pathname,
        });
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname.startsWith('/emr-assets/')) {
        if (!session) return sendJson(response, 401, { error: 'Authentication required.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const assetName = pathname.slice('/emr-assets/'.length);
        if (!emrAssets.has(assetName)) return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        const [filename, contentType] = emrAssets.get(assetName);
        return sendFile(response, path.join(config.protectedDirectory, 'assets', filename), contentType, {
          legacy: true,
          secure: config.cookieSecure,
        }, true);
      }
      if (request.method === 'GET' && pathname === '/favicon.ico') {
        applySecurityHeaders(response, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
        response.writeHead(204);
        return response.end();
      }
      return sendJson(response, 404, { error: 'Not found.' }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    } catch (error) {
      const status = Number.isInteger(error.status) ? error.status : 500;
      if (status >= 500) {
        reqLogger.error(`Unhandled error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { error, status });
      } else if (status >= 400) {
        reqLogger.warn(`Client error on ${request.method} ${pathname}: ${error.message}`, { status });
      }
      return sendJson(response, status, {
        error: status === 500 ? 'The server could not complete the request.' : error.message,
      }, { secure: config.cookieSecure });
    }
  });

  server.requestTimeout = 30_000;
  server.headersTimeout = 15_000;
  server.keepAliveTimeout = 5_000;
  server.once('listening', () => npca.start());
  server.on('close', () => npca.stop());
  server.on('close', () => diagnosisSearch.close());
  return {
    server,
    database,
    config,
    persistence,
    recovery,
    npca,
    access: {
      getUserImagingSubtypes: (userId) => getUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId),
      setUserImagingSubtypes: (userId, subtypes, actorId) => setUserImagingSubtypes(database, userId, subtypes, actorId),
    }
  };
}

export async function startServer() {
  const emrMode = (process.env.EMR_MODE || '').toLowerCase();
  const demoMode = emrMode === 'demo';
  const host = process.env.HOST || '127.0.0.1';
  const application = createEmrServer({ demoMode, host });
  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    application.server.once('error', reject);
    application.server.listen(application.config.port, application.config.host, resolve);
  });
  const modeLabel = application.config.demoMode ? 'DEMO (username-only)' : 'PRODUCTION (password required)';
  console.log(`Borkeo EMR v${APP_VERSION} [${modeLabel}] listening on http://${application.config.host}:${application.config.port}`);
  if (application.config.host === '0.0.0.0') {
    try {
      const nets = os.networkInterfaces();
      for (const name of Object.keys(nets)) {
        for (const net of nets[name] || []) {
          if (net.family === 'IPv4' && !net.internal && !net.address.startsWith('169.254')) {
            console.log(`Mobile / Phone access (Same Wi-Fi): http://${net.address}:${application.config.port}`);
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  } else {
    console.log(`Listening on local loopback address ${application.config.host} only (not exposed to network).`);
  }
  if (application.config.demoMode) {
    console.log(`DEMO: username-only accounts, localhost only. Demo data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    console.log('Demo usernames: admin, registration, triage, doctor_medicine, pharmacy.');
  }
  if (application.config.dataDirectory) console.log(`Persistent EMR data (DATA_DIR): ${application.config.dataDirectory}`);
  if (application.config.autoDatabase) {
    console.log(`Persistent EMR data: ${application.config.databasePath}`);
    if (application.persistence.sourcePath) console.log(`Initial database recovered from: ${application.persistence.sourcePath}`);
    if (application.recovery.patients || application.recovery.forms) console.log(`Recovered ${application.recovery.patients} patient record(s) and ${application.recovery.forms} clinical form(s) from ${application.recovery.sources} older database(s).`);
  }

  const cleanup = setInterval(() => application.database.deleteExpiredSessions(Date.now()), 30 * 60_000);
  cleanup.unref();
  // v41.6: unacknowledged critical results go to the ward doctors and ward head (every minute).
  const escalation = setInterval(() => {
    try { escalateCriticalResults(application.database, clinicalOptions().criticalEscalation); } catch (error) { console.error('Critical result escalation failed:', error.message); }
  }, 60_000);
  escalation.unref();
  const backupScheduler = startBackupScheduler(application.database, application.config, { backupDirectory: application.config.backupDirectory || undefined });
  const stop = () => application.server.close(() => {
    backupScheduler.stop();
    application.database.close();
    process.exit(0);
  });
  process.once('SIGINT', stop);
  process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
  return application;
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === path.resolve(currentFile)) {
  startServer().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}

export { Logger, defaultLogger, checkDatabaseIntegrity, startBackupScheduler };

import fs from 'node:fs';

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import {createEmrServer} from '../server/server.mjs';
import {validPatientPhoto, MAX_PATIENT_PHOTO_CHARS} from '../server/patient-photo.mjs';

const jpeg = 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([255,216,255,224]),Buffer.alloc(30_000, 42)]).toString('base64');
test('bounded photo validator accepts large camera JPEG and legacy PNG/WebP, rejects unsafe or oversized values', () => {
  assert.ok(jpeg.length > 20_000);
  assert.equal(validPatientPhoto(jpeg), true);
  assert.equal(validPatientPhoto(''), true);
  assert.equal(validPatientPhoto('data:image/png;base64,' + Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10]).toString('base64')), true);
  assert.equal(validPatientPhoto('data:image/webp;base64,' + Buffer.from('RIFF0000WEBP').toString('base64')), true);
  for (const bad of ['https://example.com/photo.jpg','data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zz4=','data:image/jpeg;base64,aGVsbG8=','data:image/jpeg;base64,!!!!', jpeg+'\0',true,null,'data:image/jpeg;base64,'+'A'.repeat(MAX_PATIENT_PHOTO_CHARS)]) assert.equal(validPatientPhoto(bad), false);
});

      return {Cookie,Origin:origin,'Content-Type':'application/json','X-CSRF-Token':me.csrfToken};

    const request=(url,method,headers,body)=>fetch(origin+url,{method,headers,body:JSON.stringify(body)});
    const saved=await request('/api/patient-record','PUT',registration,{patientKey,summary,data:{...photoData,'scope:registration:id:emr_pmrs_case_new':true}});
    assert.equal(saved.status,200,await saved.clone().text());assert.equal((await saved.json()).patient.photo,jpeg);

    assert.equal(referred.status,200,await referred.clone().text());

    const stored=JSON.parse(app.database.findPatient(patientKey).record_json);
    assert.equal(stored['scope:registration:id:web_patient_photo'],jpeg);
    const imaging = await login('imaging');
    const scheduledAt=Date.now()+3_600_000;
    const ordered=await request('/api/paraclinic/orders','POST',admin,{patientKey,modality:'XRAY',priority:'URGENT',request:{examination:'Chest',clinicalIndication:'Synthetic scheduling test',preferredAppointmentLocal:'2026-10-12T09:00'}});
    assert.equal(ordered.status,201,await ordered.clone().text());const imagingOrder=(await ordered.json()).order;
    const booked=await request(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${imagingOrder.id}`,'PATCH',imaging,{status:'SCHEDULED',scheduledAt});
    assert.equal(booked.status,200,await booked.clone().text());assert.equal((await booked.json()).order.scheduledAt,scheduledAt);
    const beforeRelease=app.database.findPatient(patientKey).record_json;
    const labOrderResponse=await request('/api/paraclinic/orders','POST',admin,{patientKey,modality:'LAB',priority:'ROUTINE',request:{items:[{code:'HGB'}]}});
    assert.equal(labOrderResponse.status,201,await labOrderResponse.clone().text());const labOrder=(await labOrderResponse.json()).order;
    const released=await request(`/api/paraclinic/orders/${labOrder.id}`,'PATCH',admin,{status:'PROCESSING',release:true,result:{items:[{code:'HGB',value:13.2,unit:'g/dL'}]}});
    assert.equal(released.status,200,await released.clone().text());const releasedData=await released.json();
    assert.equal(releasedData.investigationText,'');assert.equal(releasedData.order.result.items.find(item=>item.code==='HGB').value,13.2);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(app.database.findPatient(patientKey).record_json)['scope:medical:id:ipd_investigations'],JSON.parse(beforeRelease)['scope:medical:id:ipd_investigations']);
    const bad=await request('/api/patient-record','PUT',registration,{patientKey:'case:PHOTO290-BAD',summary:{...summary,caseNumber:'PHOTO290-BAD'},data:{'scope:registration:id:web_patient_photo':'data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2Zz4='}});
    assert.equal(bad.status,400);
    const longText=await request('/api/patient-record','PUT',registration,{patientKey:'case:PHOTO290-TEXT',summary:{...summary,caseNumber:'PHOTO290-TEXT'},data:{'scope:registration:id:web_phone':'x'.repeat(20_001)}});
    assert.equal(longText.status,400,'ordinary text limit remains unchanged');
  } finally { await new Promise(resolve=>app.server.close(resolve));app.database.close(); }
});

const source=fs.readFileSync(new URL('../public/paraclinic.js',import.meta.url),'utf8');
function functions() {
  const ctx={Date,Set,Map,catalog:[{code:'HGB',name:'Hemoglobin',category:'Hematology'}]};
  let start=source.indexOf('  function pendingOrder('),end=source.indexOf('  function setPatient(',start);
  // Extract helper functions by the next function boundary; esc is an arrow.
  let code=source.slice(source.indexOf('  const esc ='),source.indexOf('  const SHORT_LABEL_CODES'));
  code+=source.slice(source.indexOf('  const SHORT_LABEL_CODES'),source.indexOf('  function isWardNursingRole'));
  for (const name of ['modalityLabel','categoryLabel','groupLabItems','labRequestName']) {
    const i=source.indexOf('  function '+name+'('),j=source.indexOf('\n  ',source.indexOf('\n  }',i)+5);
    code+=source.slice(i,j);
  }
  vm.runInNewContext(code+source.slice(start,end)+'\nthis.pendingOrder=pendingOrder;this.appointmentLabel=appointmentLabel;this.pendingBanner=pendingBanner;',ctx);
  return ctx;
}
test('pending banner distinguishes pending, cancelled and completed tests and includes confirmed Imaging appointment', () => {
  const ctx=functions();
  const order={modality:'XRAY',status:'SCHEDULED',scheduledAt:Date.UTC(2026,9,12,2),request:{examination:'Chest'},result:{findings:'MUST NOT ENTER REQUEST'}};
  assert.equal(ctx.pendingOrder(order),true);
  assert.match(ctx.appointmentLabel(order),/Appointment/);
  assert.match(ctx.pendingBanner([order]),/Chest/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(ctx.pendingBanner([order]),/MUST NOT ENTER REQUEST/);
  assert.equal(ctx.pendingOrder({...order,status:'CANCELLED'}),false);
  assert.equal(ctx.pendingOrder({...order,releasedAt:Date.now()}),false);
  assert.match(ctx.appointmentLabel({...order,scheduledAt:null,request:{preferredAppointmentLocal:'2026-10-12T09:00'}}),/not confirmed/);
});

test('Paraclinic setup adds inline controls without wrapping or moving the medical form', () => {
  const setup=source.slice(source.indexOf('  function setup()'),source.indexOf('  async function openRequestChooser'));
  assert.doesNotMatch(setup,/paraclinic-split|paraclinic-panel|while \(host.firstChild\)/);
  assert.match(setup,/insertAdjacentElement\('afterend', panel\)/);
  assert.match(source,/data-branch="lab"/);assert.match(source,/data-branch="imaging"/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(source,/value="\$\{esc\(latestLabValue/);
});

  const ctx=functions();let markup='';
  ctx.document={documentElement:{lang:'km'}};ctx.patient={patientKey:'case:CHOOSER290'};ctx.refresh=async()=>{};ctx.alert=message=>{throw Error(message)};
  ctx.modal=(title,body)=>{markup=body;return {body:{querySelectorAll:()=>[]},close(){}}};

  await ctx.choose('ipd');

  assert.match(markup,/data-branch="lab"/);assert.match(markup,/data-branch="imaging"/);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');


test('v30.4 lab materials toolbar is strictly isolated from imaging films toolbar', () => {
  const stockHtml = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'protected', 'stock.html'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(stockHtml, /class="lab-toolbar workspace-toolbar material-field"/);
  assert.match(stockHtml, /class="imaging-toolbar workspace-toolbar film-field"/);
  assert.match(stockHtml, /\.imaging-toolbar\{display:none!important\}/);

  const workspaceCss = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'public', 'paraclinic-workspace.css'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(workspaceCss, /\.workspace-toolbar\[hidden\]/);
  assert.match(workspaceCss, /\.film-field\[hidden\]/);
  assert.match(workspaceCss, /display:\s*none\s*!important/);

  const imagingCss = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'public', 'imaging.css'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(imagingCss, /\.imaging-toolbar\[hidden\]/);

  const stockJs = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'public', 'stock.js'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(stockJs, /x\.style\.display = 'none'/);
  assert.match(stockJs, /x\.remove\(\)/);
});

test('v30.4 imaging catalog schema, seeding, and activation toggling', async () => {
  const { openDatabase } = await import('../server/database.mjs');
  const { ensureParaclinicSchema, imagingCatalog, updateImagingModality } = await import('../server/paraclinic.mjs');
  
  const testDbPath = path.join(rootDir, 'data', `test-imaging-catalog-${Date.now()}.sqlite`);
  const db = openDatabase(testDbPath);
  try {
    ensureParaclinicSchema(db);
    
    // Check initial seeding
    const all = imagingCatalog(db, true);
    assert.ok(all.length >= 13, 'At least 13 standard modalities seeded');
    assert.ok(all.some(m => m.code === 'ULTRASOUND' && m.active === true));
    assert.ok(all.some(m => m.code === 'MRI' && m.active === true));

    // Deactivate MRI
    const updated = updateImagingModality(db, 'MRI', { active: false });
    assert.strictEqual(updated.active, false);

    // Active-only list should exclude MRI
    const activeOnly = imagingCatalog(db, false);
    assert.ok(!activeOnly.some(m => m.code === 'MRI'), 'Inactive MRI excluded from active-only list');
    assert.ok(activeOnly.some(m => m.code === 'ULTRASOUND'), 'Active Ultrasound remains');

    // Reactivate MRI
    updateImagingModality(db, 'MRI', { active: true });
    const reactivated = imagingCatalog(db, false);
    assert.ok(reactivated.some(m => m.code === 'MRI'), 'Reactivated MRI appears again');
  } finally {
    db.close();
    try { fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath); } catch {}
  }
});

test('v30.4 lab catalog activation permission for LAB role and active filtering', () => {
  const serverCode = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'server', 'server.mjs'), 'utf8');
  // LAB role allowed set must contain 'active'
  assert.match(serverCode, /sessionRole\(session\)==='LAB'[\s\S]*?'active'/);
  assert.match(serverCode, /\/api\/imaging-catalog/);

  const labJs = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'public', 'lab.js'), 'utf8');
  assert.match(labJs, /form\.elements\[key\]\.disabled = true/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(labJs, /'active'\]\.disabled = true/);
});

test('v30.4 laboratory master-detail and grouped imaging layout', () => {
  const paraclinicJs = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootDir, 'public', 'paraclinic.js'), 'utf8');
  
  // Laboratory order master-detail layout
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /data-cat-select/);
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /data-active-category-title/);
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /rawTests\.filter\(test => test\.active !== false\)/);

  // Imaging request master-detail layout
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /imaging-order-group/);
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /imaging-test-name/);
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /data-order/);
  assert.match(paraclinicJs, /\/api\/imaging-catalog/);
});

test('v30.4 thirty patients in demo.sqlite preserve demographic and photo integrity', async () => {
  const { openDatabase } = await import('../server/database.mjs');
  const db = openDatabase(path.join(rootDir, 'data', 'demo.sqlite'));
  try {
    const rows = db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT patient_key, full_name, sex, age, record_json FROM patient_records WHERE patient_key LIKE 'case:BK-%'").all();
    assert.strictEqual(rows.length, 30);
    for (const row of rows) {
      assert.ok(row.full_name && row.full_name.trim().length > 0);
      assert.ok(row.sex === 'ប្រុស' || row.sex === 'ស្រី');
      assert.ok(Number(row.age) > 0);
      const rec = JSON.parse(row.record_json);
      const photo = rec.photo || rec['scope:registration:id:web_patient_photo'] || rec['system:patientPhoto'];
      assert.ok(photo && photo.startsWith('data:image/jpeg;base64,'));
    }
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
});

test('v30.5 demo sqlite patient database integrity and demographics preserve', async () => {
  const { openDatabase } = await import('../server/database.mjs');
  const dbPath = path.join(rootDir, 'data', 'demo.sqlite');
  if (fs.existsSync(dbPath)) {
    const db = openDatabase(dbPath);
    try {
      const rows = db.sqlite.prepare('SELECT patient_key, full_name, sex, age FROM patient_records').all();
      assert.ok(rows.length >= 30, 'All 30 patient records are intact');
      for (const row of rows.slice(0, 5)) {
        assert.ok(row.full_name, 'Name is preserved');
        assert.ok(row.sex, 'Sex is preserved');
      }
    } finally {
      db.close();
    }
  }
});
